Replacement Suggestions

ContentsTopPrevious: Retire-Now ContentNext: Disabled Content

Best algorithmic successor candidate for each row. Candidates require governance confirmation before retirement.

What Drives The Replacement Score

5 variables

The stored field is replacement_similarity_score. It is mostly about content similarity, not title similarity.

0.55 * content_similarity + 0.15 * category_similarity + 0.15 * recency_similarity + 0.10 * level_similarity + 0.05 * title_similarity

Higher is better. A higher score means the candidate is a stronger replacement match.

Content Similarity: 55%Category Similarity: 15%Recency Similarity: 15%Level Similarity: 10%Title Similarity: 5%
content_similarityHow much the real markdown content overlaps after stripping acknowledgement noise.
category_similarityExtra credit when both items sit in the same category.
recency_similarityExtra credit when the candidate is newer or close in age.
level_similarityExtra credit when the workshop level is aligned.
title_similaritySmall tie-breaker only. It does not drive the result.
  • Disabled rows, events, and unavailable rows are excluded before scoring starts.
  • Workshops only compare to workshops. Sprints only compare to sprints.
  • The scorer uses actual markdown content when it can. Metadata-only matches are not treated as strong replacements.

Show Replacement List

100 rows

Each row expands to show the recommended replacement details, including owner and contact fields when they exist.

100 rowsClick a row for more details. If markdown file checks are available, they open inside the row details.
